  # 使用者問題                                                                 
  question =                                                                   
  "請整理2013至2017年間敘利亞化學武器情況，逐條引用並註明來源，製作總結報告，  
  包含趨勢分析與SWOT分析。"                                                    
                                                                               
  # 1. 解析核心關鍵字 (core_terms) 與時間範圍                                  
  core_terms = ["敘利亞", "化學武器"]          #                               
  只保留主題與地點，排除時間字串                                               
  core_query = f"核心關鍵詞組如下：{'、'.join(core_terms)}"                    
                                                                               
  date_from = "2013-01-01"                                                     
  date_to   = "2017-12-31"                                                     
                                                                               
  # 2. 依 core_query 於 Elasticsearch 搜尋新聞                                 
  search_result = es_search_news(                                              
      query=core_query,                                                        
      must_high_value=False,                                                   
      must_selected=False,                                                     
      size=10000,                                                              
      date_from=date_from,                                                     
      date_to=date_to,                                                         
      selected_boost=6.0,                                                      
      high_value_boost=2.5,                                                    
      recency_boost=False,                                                     
      recency_field="importDT",                                                
      recency_scale="30d",                                                     
      index_hint="news_test"                                                   
  )                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 取得 evidence_snippets                                                     
  evidence_snippets = search_result.get("evidence_snippets", [])               
                                                                               
  # 3. 統計時間分布                                                            
  aggregation = aggregate_month_quarter(evidence_snippets=evidence_snippets)   
                                                                               
  doc_count      = aggregation.get("doc_count", 0)                             
  snippet_count  = aggregation.get("snippet_count", 0)                         
  active_days    = aggregation.get("active_days", 0)                           
  day_counts     = aggregation.get("day_counts", {})                           
  month_counts   = aggregation.get("month_counts", {})                         
  quarter_counts = aggregation.get("quarter_counts", {})                       
                                                                               
  # 4. 若有時間範圍，進一步過濾統計結果                                        
  start_date = date_from                                                       
  end_date   = date_to                                                         
                                                                               
  if start_date or end_date:                                                   
      # 過濾 day_counts                                                        
      filtered_day_counts = {}                                                 
      for d, cnt in day_counts.items():                                        
          if start_date and d < start_date:                                    
              continue                                                         
          if end_date and d > end_date:                                        
              continue                                                         
          filtered_day_counts[d] = cnt                                         
      day_counts = filtered_day_counts                                         
                                                                               
      # 過濾 month_counts（保留完全落在範圍內的月份）                          
      filtered_month_counts = {}                                               
      for m, cnt in month_counts.items():  # m 形如 "YYYY-MM"                  
          m_year = m[0:4]                                                      
          m_month = m[5:7]                                                     
          m_first = f"{m_year}-{m_month}-01"                                   
          m_last  = f"{m_year}-{m_month}-31"   # 簡化處理                      
          if start_date and m_last < start_date:                               
              continue                                                         
          if end_date and m_first > end_date:                                  
              continue                                                         
          filtered_month_counts[m] = cnt                                       
      month_counts = filtered_month_counts                                     
                                                                               
      # 過濾 quarter_counts（形如 "YYYY-Qn"）                                  
      filtered_quarter_counts = {}                                             
      for q, cnt in quarter_counts.items():                                    
          q_year = q[0:4]                                                      
          q_id = q[-1]   # '1'..'4'                                            
          if q_id == "1":                                                      
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-01-01", f"{q_year}-03-31"           
          elif q_id == "2":                                                    
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-04-01", f"{q_year}-06-30"           
          elif q_id == "3":                                                    
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-07-01", f"{q_year}-09-30"           
          else:                                                                
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-10-01", f"{q_year}-12-31"           
                                                                               
          if start_date and q_last < start_date:                               
              continue                                                         
          if end_date and q_first > end_date:                                  
              continue                                                         
          filtered_quarter_counts[q] = cnt                                     
      quarter_counts = filtered_quarter_counts                                 
                                                                               
  # 5. 組合 query_meta                                                         
  search_qm = (search_result.get("query_meta") or {}) if                       
  isinstance(search_result, dict) else {}                                      
                                                                               
  query_meta = {                                                               
      "core_terms": core_terms,                                                
      "core_query": core_query,                                                
      "date_from": date_from,                                                  
      "date_to": date_to,                                                      
      "syn_terms": search_qm.get("syn_terms"),                                 
      "effective_terms": search_qm.get("effective_terms"),                     
      "es_query": search_qm.get("es_query"),                                   
      "manual_terms_in_q": search_qm.get("manual_terms_in_q"),                 
      "is_manual_entity_query": search_qm.get("is_manual_entity_query"),       
      "term_groups": search_qm.get("term_groups"),                             
      "raw_query": search_qm.get("query"),                                     
  }                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 6. 建立 payload                                                            
  payload = {                                                                  
      "question": question,                                                    
      "time_range": {                                                          
          "date_from": date_from,                                              
          "date_to": date_to,                                                  
      },                                                                       
      "stats": {                                                               
          "doc_count": doc_count,                                              
          "snippet_count": snippet_count,                                      
          "active_days": active_days,                                          
          "day_counts": day_counts,                                            
          "month_counts": month_counts,                                        
          "quarter_counts": quarter_counts,                                    
      },                                                                       
      "evidence_snippets": evidence_snippets,                                  
      "raw_search_result": search_result,                                      
      "query_meta": query_meta,                                                
  }                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 7. 回傳結果                                                                
  final_answer({                                                               
      "payload": payload                                                       
  })

  # 使用者問題                                                                 
  question =                                                                   
  "綜整2024年第四季BM-30與咆哮者的衛照掌握動態，於烏克蘭防守角度進行SWOT分析   
  ，並產製以日期為縱軸、活動為橫軸的二維樞紐統計表。"                          
                                                                               
  # 1. 解析核心關鍵詞 (core_terms) 與時間範圍                                  
  core_terms = ["BM-30", "咆哮者", "烏克蘭"]  #                                
  依規則排除時間相關字串與報告語氣詞                                           
  core_query = f"核心關鍵詞組如下：{'、'.join(core_terms)}"                    
                                                                               
  # 時間範圍：2024年第四季 => 2024-10-01 ~ 2024-12-31                          
  date_from = "2024-10-01"                                                     
  date_to   = "2024-12-31"                                                     
                                                                               
  # 2. 呼叫 ES 搜尋                                                            
  search_result = es_search_news(                                              
      query=core_query,                                                        
      must_high_value=False,                                                   
      must_selected=False,                                                     
      size=10000,                                                              
      date_from=date_from,                                                     
      date_to=date_to,                                                         
      selected_boost=6.0,                                                      
      high_value_boost=2.5,                                                    
      recency_boost=False,                                                     
      recency_field="importDT",                                                
      recency_scale="30d",                                                     
      index_hint="mil_reports-01,nav_intel_sentences_synthetic_v1,satmeta"     
  )                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 取得 evidence_snippets                                                     
  evidence_snippets = search_result.get("evidence_snippets", [])               
                                                                               
  # 3. 統計彙總                                                                
  aggregation = aggregate_month_quarter(evidence_snippets=evidence_snippets)   
  doc_count      = aggregation.get("doc_count", 0)                             
  snippet_count  = aggregation.get("snippet_count", 0)                         
  active_days    = aggregation.get("active_days", 0)                           
  day_counts     = aggregation.get("day_counts", {})                           
  month_counts   = aggregation.get("month_counts", {})                         
  quarter_counts = aggregation.get("quarter_counts", {})                       
                                                                               
  # 4. 若有時間範圍，進一步過濾 day/month/quarter 統計                         
  start_date = date_from                                                       
  end_date   = date_to                                                         
                                                                               
  if start_date or end_date:                                                   
      # 過濾 day_counts                                                        
      filtered_day_counts = {}                                                 
      for d, cnt in day_counts.items():                                        
          if start_date and d < start_date:                                    
              continue                                                         
          if end_date and d > end_date:                                        
              continue                                                         
          filtered_day_counts[d] = cnt                                         
      day_counts = filtered_day_counts                                         
                                                                               
      # 過濾 month_counts（保留完全落在範圍內的月份）                          
      filtered_month_counts = {}                                               
      for m, cnt in month_counts.items():  # m 形如 "2024-10"                  
          m_year = m[0:4]                                                      
          m_month = m[5:7]                                                     
          m_first = f"{m_year}-{m_month}-01"                                   
          m_last  = f"{m_year}-{m_month}-31"  # 簡化處理                       
          if start_date and m_last < start_date:                               
              continue                                                         
          if end_date and m_first > end_date:                                  
              continue                                                         
          filtered_month_counts[m] = cnt                                       
      month_counts = filtered_month_counts                                     
                                                                               
      # 過濾 quarter_counts（形如 "2024-Q4"）                                  
      filtered_quarter_counts = {}                                             
      for q, cnt in quarter_counts.items():                                    
          q_year = q[0:4]                                                      
          q_id = q[-1]  # "1".."4"                                             
          if q_id == "1":                                                      
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-01-01", f"{q_year}-03-31"           
          elif q_id == "2":                                                    
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-04-01", f"{q_year}-06-30"           
          elif q_id == "3":                                                    
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-07-01", f"{q_year}-09-30"           
          else:  # q_id == "4"                                                 
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-10-01", f"{q_year}-12-31"           
                                                                               
          if start_date and q_last < start_date:                               
              continue                                                         
          if end_date and q_first > end_date:                                  
              continue                                                         
          filtered_quarter_counts[q] = cnt                                     
      quarter_counts = filtered_quarter_counts                                 
                                                                               
  # 5. 組合 query_meta                                                         
  search_qm = (search_result.get("query_meta") or {}) if                       
  isinstance(search_result, dict) else {}                                      
  query_meta = {                                                               
      "core_terms": core_terms,                                                
      "core_query": core_query,                                                
      "date_from": date_from,                                                  
      "date_to": date_to,                                                      
      "syn_terms": search_qm.get("syn_terms"),                                 
      "effective_terms": search_qm.get("effective_terms"),                     
      "es_query": search_qm.get("es_query"),                                   
      "manual_terms_in_q": search_qm.get("manual_terms_in_q"),                 
      "is_manual_entity_query": search_qm.get("is_manual_entity_query"),       
      "term_groups": search_qm.get("term_groups"),                             
      "raw_query": search_qm.get("query"),                                     
  }                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 6. 建立 payload                                                            
  payload = {                                                                  
      "question": question,                                                    
      "time_range": {                                                          
          "date_from": date_from,                                              
          "date_to": date_to,                                                  
      },                                                                       
      "stats": {                                                               
          "doc_count": doc_count,                                              
          "snippet_count": snippet_count,                                      
          "active_days": active_days,                                          
          "day_counts": day_counts,                                            
          "month_counts": month_counts,                                        
          "quarter_counts": quarter_counts,                                    
      },                                                                       
      "evidence_snippets": evidence_snippets,                                  
      "raw_search_result": search_result,                                      
      "query_meta": query_meta,                                                
  }                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 7. 回傳結果                                                                
  final_answer({                                                               
      "payload": payload                                                       
  })

  # 1. 取得使用者問題                                                          
  question =                                                                   
  "我得到一則情報指出2025年11月20日下午4點在馬達加斯加海域發現共軍D52大驅在進  
  行護船，防海盜，請以聯合情監的風格寫成一則軍事情報。"                        
  original_article = question                                                  
                                                                               
  # 2. 手動建立 style_terms（風格相關關鍵字）                                  
  style_terms = ["聯合情監", "軍事情報"]                                       
                                                                               
  # 3. 從問題中萃取 topic_terms（排除時間資訊）                                
  #    只保留與主題、領域相關的關鍵字                                          
  topic_terms = ["馬達加斯加", "D52大驅", "護船", "防海盜"]                    
                                                                               
  # 4. 組合查詢字串                                                            
  primary_terms = style_terms + topic_terms                                    
  primary_query = f"風格仿寫關鍵詞組如下：{'、'.join(primary_terms)}" if       
  primary_terms else "公文"                                                    
  fallback_query = f"風格仿寫關鍵詞組如下：{'、'.join(style_terms)}" if        
  style_terms else "公文"                                                      
                                                                               
  # 5. 取得系統提供的 index_hint                                               
  index_hint = "mil_reports-01,nav_intel_sentences_synthetic_v1,satmeta"       
                                                                               
  # 6. 先用 primary_query 搜尋                                                 
  search_result = es_search_news(                                              
      query=primary_query,                                                     
      must_high_value=False,                                                   
      must_selected=False,                                                     
      size=20,                                                                 
      date_from=None,                                                          
      date_to=None,                                                            
      selected_boost=2.0,                                                      
      high_value_boost=1.5,                                                    
      recency_boost=False,                                                     
      recency_field="importDT",                                                
      recency_scale="365d",                                                    
      index_hint=index_hint,                                                   
  )                                                                            
                                                                               
  hits = (search_result.get("hits") or [])                                     
  evidence_snippets = (search_result.get("evidence_snippets") or [])           
                                                                               
  # 若命中文件太少，改用 fallback_query                                        
  if len(hits) < 5:                                                            
      search_result = es_search_news(                                          
          query=fallback_query,                                                
          must_high_value=False,                                               
          must_selected=False,                                                 
          size=20,                                                             
          date_from=None,                                                      
          date_to=None,                                                        
          selected_boost=2.0,                                                  
          high_value_boost=1.5,                                                
          recency_boost=False,                                                 
          recency_field="importDT",                                            
          recency_scale="365d",                                                
          index_hint=index_hint,                                               
      )                                                                        
      hits = (search_result.get("hits") or [])                                 
      evidence_snippets = (search_result.get("evidence_snippets") or [])       
                                                                               
  # 記錄實際使用的查詢字串                                                     
  style_query = primary_query if len(hits) >= 5 else fallback_query            
                                                                               
  # 7. 依 uuid 抓全文，組成 style_docs（最多 8 篇）                            
  style_docs = []                                                              
  for h in hits[:8]:                                                           
      uuid = h.get("uuid")                                                     
      if not uuid:                                                             
          continue                                                             
      doc = es_fetch_doc_by_id(                                                
          uuid=uuid,                                                           
          index_hint=index_hint,                                               
          text_field="full_text",  # 若索引使用其他欄位，可自行調整            
      )                                                                        
      full_text = (doc or {}).get("full_text") or ""                           
      if not full_text:                                                        
          continue                                                             
      # 限制長度避免過長                                                       
      if len(full_text) > 4000:                                                
          full_text = full_text[:4000]                                         
      style_docs.append({                                                      
          "uuid": uuid,                                                        
          "subject": h.get("subject"),                                         
          "article_title": h.get("article_title"),                             
          "full_text": full_text,                                              
      })                                                                       
                                                                               
  # 8. 取得查詢的 meta 資訊                                                    
  search_qm = (search_result.get("query_meta") or {}) if                       
  isinstance(search_result, dict) else {}                                      
                                                                               
  core_terms = style_terms + topic_terms                                       
  date_from = None                                                             
  date_to = None                                                               
                                                                               
  query_meta = {                                                               
      "style_terms": style_terms,                                              
      "topic_terms": topic_terms,                                              
      "core_terms": core_terms,                                                
      "core_query": style_query,                                               
      "date_from": date_from,                                                  
      "date_to": date_to,                                                      
      "syn_terms": search_qm.get("syn_terms"),                                 
      "effective_terms": search_qm.get("effective_terms"),                     
      "es_query": search_qm.get("es_query"),                                   
      "manual_terms_in_q": search_qm.get("manual_terms_in_q"),                 
      "is_manual_entity_query": search_qm.get("is_manual_entity_query"),       
      "term_groups": search_qm.get("term_groups"),                             
      "raw_query": search_qm.get("query"),                                     
  }                                                                            
                                                                               
  payload = {                                                                  
      "question": question,                                                    
      "original_article": original_article,                                    
      "style_query": style_query,                                              
      "style_terms": style_terms,                                              
      "topic_terms": topic_terms,                                              
      "core_terms": core_terms,                                                
      "date_from": date_from,                                                  
      "date_to": date_to,                                                      
      "stats": {                                                               
          "doc_count": len(hits),                                              
          "snippet_count": len(evidence_snippets),                             
          "full_doc_count": len(style_docs),                                   
      },                                                                       
      "evidence_snippets": evidence_snippets,                                  
      "style_docs": style_docs,                                                
      "query_meta": query_meta,                                                
      "_display_info": {                                                       
          "hits_preview": [                                                    
              {                                                                
                  "uuid": h.get("uuid"),                                       
                  "article_title": h.get("article_title"),                     
                  "subject": h.get("subject"),                                 
                  "article_date_iso": h.get("article_date_iso"),               
              }                                                                
              for h in hits[:20]                                               
          ]                                                                    
      }                                                                        
  }                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 9. 回傳 payload                                                            
  final_answer({                                                               
      "payload": payload                                                       
  })

  # 使用者問題                                                                 
  question =                                                                   
  "請綜整2024年第四季BM-30與咆哮者的衛星偵測動態，從烏克蘭防守角度做SWOT分析   
  ，並製作縱軸為日期、橫軸為活動的二維樞紐統計表，於表格數值後列出前五筆來源   
  （1、2、3…）。"                                                              
                                                                               
  # 1. 解析核心關鍵詞（不含時間）                                              
  core_terms = ["BM-30", "咆哮者", "烏克蘭"]                                   
  core_query = f"核心關鍵詞組如下：{'、'.join(core_terms)}"                    
                                                                               
  # 2. 解析時間範圍                                                            
  date_from = "2024-10-01"   # 2024 年第四季起始                               
  date_to   = "2024-12-31"   # 2024 年第四季結束                               
                                                                               
  # 3. 系統提供的 index_hint                                                   
  index_hint = "mil_reports-01,nav_intel_sentences_synthetic_v1,satmeta"       
                                                                               
  # 4. 呼叫 ES 搜尋                                                            
  search_result = es_search_news(                                              
      query=core_query,                                                        
      must_high_value=False,                                                   
      must_selected=False,                                                     
      size=10000,                                                              
      date_from=date_from,                                                     
      date_to=date_to,                                                         
      selected_boost=6.0,                                                      
      high_value_boost=2.5,                                                    
      recency_boost=False,                                                     
      recency_field="importDT",                                                
      recency_scale="30d",                                                     
      index_hint=index_hint,                                                   
  )                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 5. 取得 evidence snippets                                                  
  evidence_snippets = search_result.get("evidence_snippets", [])               
                                                                               
  # 6. 時間統計聚合                                                            
  aggregation = aggregate_month_quarter(evidence_snippets=evidence_snippets)   
                                                                               
  doc_count = aggregation.get("doc_count", 0)                                  
  snippet_count = aggregation.get("snippet_count", 0)                          
  active_days = aggregation.get("active_days", 0)                              
  day_counts = aggregation.get("day_counts", {})                               
  month_counts = aggregation.get("month_counts", {})                           
  quarter_counts = aggregation.get("quarter_counts", {})                       
                                                                               
  # 7. 依時間範圍過濾統計結果                                                  
  if date_from or date_to:                                                     
      # day_counts                                                             
      filtered_day_counts = {}                                                 
      for d, cnt in day_counts.items():                                        
          if date_from and d < date_from:                                      
              continue                                                         
          if date_to and d > date_to:                                          
              continue                                                         
          filtered_day_counts[d] = cnt                                         
      day_counts = filtered_day_counts                                         
                                                                               
      # month_counts (格式 "YYYY-MM")                                          
      filtered_month_counts = {}                                               
      for m, cnt in month_counts.items():                                      
          m_year = m[0:4]                                                      
          m_month = m[5:7]                                                     
          m_first = f"{m_year}-{m_month}-01"                                   
          m_last = f"{m_year}-{m_month}-31"                                    
          if date_from and m_last < date_from:                                 
              continue                                                         
          if date_to and m_first > date_to:                                    
              continue                                                         
          filtered_month_counts[m] = cnt                                       
      month_counts = filtered_month_counts                                     
                                                                               
      # quarter_counts (格式 "YYYY-QX")                                        
      filtered_quarter_counts = {}                                             
      for q, cnt in quarter_counts.items():                                    
          q_year = q[0:4]                                                      
          q_id = q[-1]  # 1~4                                                  
          if q_id == "1":                                                      
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-01-01", f"{q_year}-03-31"           
          elif q_id == "2":                                                    
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-04-01", f"{q_year}-06-30"           
          elif q_id == "3":                                                    
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-07-01", f"{q_year}-09-30"           
          else:                                                                
              q_first, q_last = f"{q_year}-10-01", f"{q_year}-12-31"           
          if date_from and q_last < date_from:                                 
              continue                                                         
          if date_to and q_first > date_to:                                    
              continue                                                         
          filtered_quarter_counts[q] = cnt                                     
      quarter_counts = filtered_quarter_counts                                 
                                                                               
  # 8. 取得 ES 回傳的 query_meta（若無則空 dict）                              
  search_qm = (search_result.get("query_meta") or {}) if                       
  isinstance(search_result, dict) else {}                                      
                                                                               
  # 9. 組合 query_meta                                                         
  query_meta = {                                                               
      "core_terms": core_terms,                                                
      "core_query": core_query,                                                
      "date_from": date_from,                                                  
      "date_to": date_to,                                                      
      "syn_terms": search_qm.get("syn_terms"),                                 
      "effective_terms": search_qm.get("effective_terms"),                     
      "es_query": search_qm.get("es_query"),                                   
      "manual_terms_in_q": search_qm.get("manual_terms_in_q"),                 
      "is_manual_entity_query": search_qm.get("is_manual_entity_query"),       
      "term_groups": search_qm.get("term_groups"),                             
      "raw_query": search_qm.get("query"),                                     
  }                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 10. 組成 payload                                                           
  payload = {                                                                  
      "question": question,                                                    
      "time_range": {                                                          
          "date_from": date_from,                                              
          "date_to": date_to,                                                  
      },                                                                       
      "stats": {                                                               
          "doc_count": doc_count,                                              
          "snippet_count": snippet_count,                                      
          "active_days": active_days,                                          
          "day_counts": day_counts,                                            
          "month_counts": month_counts,                                        
          "quarter_counts": quarter_counts,                                    
      },                                                                       
      "evidence_snippets": evidence_snippets,                                  
      "raw_search_result": search_result,                                      
      "query_meta": query_meta,                                                
  }                                                                            
                                                                               
  # 11. 回傳結果                                                               
  final_answer({"payload": payload})
